```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class AmbiguousOptionExceptionTest {

    @Test
    void createMessage_shouldHandleEmptyCollection() {
        String result = AmbiguousOptionException.createMessage("opt", Collections.emptyList());
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: )", result);
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_shouldHandleSingleOption() {
        String result = AmbiguousOptionException.createMessage("opt", Collections.singletonList("opt1"));
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: 'opt1')", result);
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_shouldHandleTwoOptions() {
        Collection<String> options = Arrays.asList("opt1", "opt2");
        String result = AmbiguousOptionException.createMessage("opt", options);
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: 'opt1', 'opt2')", result);
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_shouldHandleThreeOptions() {
        Collection<String> options = Arrays.asList("opt1", "opt2", "opt3");
        String result = AmbiguousOptionException.createMessage("opt", options);
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: 'opt1', 'opt2', 'opt3')", result);
    }

    @Test
    void constructor_shouldSetPropertiesForEmptyOptions() {
        Collection<String> options = Collections.emptyList();
        AmbiguousOptionException exception = new AmbiguousOptionException("opt", options);
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: )", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(options, exception.getMatchingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructor_shouldSetPropertiesForSingleOption() {
        Collection<String> options = Collections.singletonList("opt1");
        AmbiguousOptionException exception = new AmbiguousOptionException("opt", options);
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: 'opt1')", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(options, exception.getMatchingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructor_shouldSetPropertiesForMultipleOptions() {
        Collection<String> options = Arrays.asList("opt1", "opt2");
        AmbiguousOptionException exception = new AmbiguousOptionException("opt", options);
        assertEquals("Ambiguous option: 'opt'  (could be: 'opt1', 'opt2')", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(options, exception.getMatchingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void getMatchingOptions_shouldReturnCorrectCollection() {
        Collection<String> options = Arrays.asList("a", "b");
        AmbiguousOptionException exception = new AmbiguousOptionException("test", options);
        assertEquals(options, exception.getMatchingOptions());
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.lang.reflect.Constructor;
import java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class CharTest {

    @Test
    void testPrivateConstructorAccessibility() throws NoSuchMethodException {
        Constructor<Char> constructor = Char.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        assertFalse(constructor.isAccessible());
    }

    @Test
    void testPrivateConstructorInvocation() throws NoSuchMethodException, IllegalAccessException {
        Constructor<Char> constructor = Char.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        constructor.setAccessible(true);

        assertDoesNotThrow(() -> {
            Char instance = constructor.newInstance();
            assertNotNull(instance);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void testStaticConstantsValues() {
        assertEquals('\'', Char.APOS);
        assertEquals('\r', Char.CR);
        assertEquals('=', Char.EQUAL);
        assertEquals('\n', Char.LF);
        assertEquals(' ', Char.SP);
        assertEquals('\t', Char.TAB);
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.lang.reflect.Constructor;
import java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;
import java.lang.reflect.Modifier;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class CharTest {

    @Test
    void testPrivateConstructorAccessibility() throws NoSuchMethodException {
        Constructor<Char> constructor = Char.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        assertTrue(Modifier.isPrivate(constructor.getModifiers()));
    }

    @Test
    void testPrivateConstructorInvocation() throws Exception {
        Constructor<Char> constructor = Char.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        constructor.setAccessible(true);

        InvocationTargetException exception = assertThrows(InvocationTargetException.class, constructor::newInstance);
        assertTrue(exception.getCause() instanceof AssertionError);
    }

    @Test
    void testStaticConstantsValues() {
        assertEquals('\'', Char.APOS);
        assertEquals('\r', Char.CR);
        assertEquals('=', Char.EQUAL);
        assertEquals('\n', Char.LF);
        assertEquals(' ', Char.SP);
        assertEquals('\t', Char.TAB);
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.function.Supplier;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class CommandLineTest {
    private CommandLine commandLine;
    private Option option;
    private OptionGroup optionGroup;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        option = Option.builder("t").build();
        optionGroup = new OptionGroup();
        optionGroup.addOption(option);
        optionGroup.setSelected(option);

        List<String> args = List.of("arg1", "arg2");
        List<Option> options = List.of(option);
        commandLine = new CommandLine(args, options, o -> {});
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderAddArg() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = CommandLine.builder();
        builder.addArg("test");
        assertTrue(builder.args.contains("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderAddOption() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = CommandLine.builder();
        builder.addOption(option);
        assertTrue(builder.options.contains(option));
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderSetDeprecatedHandler() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = CommandLine.builder();
        Consumer<Option> handler = o -> {};
        builder.setDeprecatedHandler(handler);
        assertEquals(handler, builder.deprecatedHandler);
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderGet() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = CommandLine.builder();
        builder.addArg("arg").addOption(option);
        CommandLine cmd = builder.get();
        assertFalse(cmd.args.isEmpty());
        assertFalse(cmd.options.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void testGetArgList() {
        List<String> argList = commandLine.getArgList();
        assertEquals(2, argList.size());
    }

    @Test
    void testGetArgs() {
        String[] args = commandLine.getArgs();
        assertEquals(2, args.length);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValueByOption() {
        option.addValue("value");
        String value = commandLine.getOptionValue(option);
        assertEquals("value", value);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValueByOptionWithDefault() {
        String value = commandLine.getOptionValue(option, "default");
        assertEquals("default", value);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValueByOptionGroup() {
        option.addValue("value");
        String value = commandLine.getOptionValue(optionGroup);
        assertEquals("value", value);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValueByOptionGroupWithDefault() {
        String value = commandLine.getOptionValue(optionGroup, "default");
        assertEquals("default", value);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValueByOptionName() {
        option.addValue("value");
        String value = commandLine.getOptionValue("t");
        assertEquals("value", value);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValuesByOption() {
        option.addValue("v1");
        option.addValue("v2");
        String[] values = commandLine.getOptionValues(option);
        assertEquals(2, values.length);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionValuesByOptionGroup() {
        option.addValue("v1");
        option.addValue("v2");
        String[] values = commandLine.getOptionValues(optionGroup);
        assertEquals(2, values.length);
    }

    @Test
    void testHasOptionByChar() {
        assertTrue(commandLine.hasOption('t'));
    }

    @Test
    void testHasOptionByOption() {
        assertTrue(commandLine.hasOption(option));
    }

    @Test
    void testHasOptionByOptionGroup() {
        assertTrue(commandLine.hasOption(optionGroup));
    }

    @Test
    void testHasOptionByName() {
        assertTrue(commandLine.hasOption("t"));
    }

    @Test
    void testIterator() {
        Iterator<Option> it = commandLine.iterator();
        assertTrue(it.hasNext());
        assertEquals(option, it.next());
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionPropertiesByOption() {
        option.addValue("key");
        option.addValue("value");
        Properties props = commandLine.getOptionProperties(option);
        assertEquals("value", props.getProperty("key"));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionPropertiesByName() {
        option.addValue("key");
        option.addValue("value");
        Properties props = commandLine.getOptionProperties("t");
        assertEquals("value", props.getProperty("key"));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetParsedOptionValue() {
        option.setConverter(Integer::parseInt);
        option.addValue("123");
        try {
            Integer value = commandLine.getParsedOptionValue(option);
            assertEquals(123, value);
        } catch (ParseException e) {
            fail("Unexpected exception");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void testGetParsedOptionValueWithDefault() {
        try {
            Integer value = commandLine.getParsedOptionValue(option, 456);
            assertEquals(456, value);
        } catch (ParseException e) {
            fail("Unexpected exception");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void testGetParsedOptionValueWithSupplierDefault() {
        try {
            Integer value = commandLine.getParsedOptionValue(option, () -> 789);
            assertEquals(789, value);
        } catch (ParseException e) {
            fail("Unexpected exception");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void testHandleDeprecated() {
        option.setDeprecated("Deprecated");
        commandLine.handleDeprecated(option);
    }

    @Test
    void testGetDeprecated() {
        option.setDeprecated("Deprecated");
        assertThrows(ParseException.class, () -> {
            commandLine.getParsedOptionValue(option);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void testNullOptionHandling() {
        assertNull(commandLine.resolveOption(null));
        assertFalse(commandLine.hasOption((Option) null));
    }

    @Test
    void testEmptyOptionValues() {
        String[] values = commandLine.getOptionValues(option);
        assertNull(values);
    }

    @Test
    void testUnselectedOptionGroup() {
        OptionGroup unselectedGroup = new OptionGroup();
        assertFalse(commandLine.hasOption(unselectedGroup));
        assertNull(commandLine.getOptionValues(unselectedGroup));
    }
}

```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.function.Supplier;
import java.util.Iterator;
import org.apache.commons.cli.ParseException;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class CommandLineTest {
    private CommandLine commandLine;
    private Option option;
    private OptionGroup optionGroup;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        option = Option.builder("t").build();
        optionGroup = new OptionGroup();
        optionGroup.addOption(option);
        optionGroup.setSelected(option);

        List<String> args = List.of("arg1", "arg2");
        List<Option> options = List.of(option);
        commandLine = new CommandLine(args, options, o -> {});
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderConstructor() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = new CommandLine.Builder();
        assertNotNull(builder);
    }

    @Test
    void testDefaultConstructor() {
        CommandLine cmd = new CommandLine();
        assertNotNull(cmd);
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderAddArg() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = CommandLine.builder();
        builder.addArg("test");
        assertTrue(builder.args.contains("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderAddOption() {
        CommandLine.Builder builder = CommandLine.builder();
        builder.addOption(option);
        assertTrue(builder.options.contains(option));

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class CommandLineParserTest {

    @Test
    public void testParseEmptyArguments() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{});
        assertFalse(cmd.hasOption("a"));
        assertEquals(0, cmd.getArgList().size());
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseSingleOption() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a"});
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseOptionWithArgument() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").hasArg().build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a", "value"});
        assertEquals("value", cmd.getOptionValue("a"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseMissingOptionArgument() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").hasArg().build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Assertions.assertThrows(ParseException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a"});
        });
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseUnrecognizedOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Assertions.assertThrows(ParseException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-b"});
        });
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseNonOptionArguments() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{"arg1", "arg2"});
        assertEquals(2, cmd.getArgList().size());
        assertEquals("arg1", cmd.getArgList().get(0));
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseMixedOptionsAndArguments() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a", "arg1"});
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
        assertEquals("arg1", cmd.getArgList().get(0));
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseStopAtNonOptionTrue() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a", "arg1", "-b"}, true);
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
        assertEquals(2, cmd.getArgList().size());
        assertEquals("arg1", cmd.getArgList().get(0));
        assertEquals("-b", cmd.getArgList().get(1));
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseStopAtNonOptionFalse() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Assertions.assertThrows(ParseException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a", "arg1", "-b"}, false);
        });
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseRequiredOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").required().build());
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Assertions.assertThrows(ParseException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, new String[]{});
        });
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseOptionGroup() throws Exception {
        Options options = new Options();
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        group.addOption(Option.builder("b").build());
        options.addOptionGroup(group);
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a"});
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
        assertFalse(cmd.hasOption("b"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testParseMutuallyExclusiveOptions() {
        Options options = new Options();
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        group.addOption(Option.builder("b").build());
        options.addOptionGroup(group);
        CommandLineParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Assertions.assertThrows(ParseException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, new String[]{"-a", "-b"});
        });
    }
}

        group.setRequired(true);
        options.addOptionGroup(group);
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        parser.options = options;
        parser.expectedOpts = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList(group));
        parser.updateRequiredOptions(Option.builder("a").build());
        assertTrue(parser.expectedOpts.isEmpty());

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class DeprecatedAttributesTest {

    @Test
    public void testBuilder() {
        DeprecatedAttributes.Builder builder = DeprecatedAttributes.builder();
        assertNotNull(builder);
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetDescription() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setDescription("test description")
                .get();
        assertEquals("test description", attributes.getDescription());
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetSince() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setSince("1.2.3")
                .get();
        assertEquals("1.2.3", attributes.getSince());
    }

    @Test
    public void testIsForRemoval() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setForRemoval(true)
                .get();
        assertTrue(attributes.isForRemoval());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToEmptyWithNull() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setDescription(null)
                .setSince(null)
                .get();
        assertEquals("", attributes.getDescription());
        assertEquals("", attributes.getSince());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToEmptyWithEmptyString() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setDescription("")
                .setSince("")
                .get();
        assertEquals("", attributes.getDescription());
        assertEquals("", attributes.getSince());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_OnlyForRemoval() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setForRemoval(true)
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated for removal", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_OnlySince() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setSince("2.0")
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated since 2.0", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_OnlyDescription() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setDescription("obsolete feature")
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated: obsolete feature", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_ForRemovalAndSince() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setForRemoval(true)
                .setSince("3.0")
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated for removal since 3.0", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_ForRemovalAndDescription() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setForRemoval(true)
                .setDescription("will be removed")
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated for removal: will be removed", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_SinceAndDescription() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setSince("4.0")
                .setDescription("deprecated in version")
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated since 4.0: deprecated in version", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString_AllFields() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setForRemoval(true)
                .setSince("5.0")
                .setDescription("complete removal")
                .get();
        assertEquals("Deprecated for removal since 5.0: complete removal", attributes.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testBuilderSetDescription() {
        DeprecatedAttributes.Builder builder = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setDescription("desc");
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = builder.get();
        assertEquals("desc", attributes.getDescription());
    }

    @Test
    public void testBuilderSetForRemoval() {
        DeprecatedAttributes.Builder builder = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setForRemoval(false);
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = builder.get();
        assertFalse(attributes.isForRemoval());
    }

    @Test
    public void testBuilderSetSince() {
        DeprecatedAttributes.Builder builder = DeprecatedAttributes.builder()
                .setSince("6.0");
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = builder.get();
        assertEquals("6.0", attributes.getSince());
    }

    @Test
    public void testBuilderGet() {
        DeprecatedAttributes.Builder builder = DeprecatedAttributes.builder();
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = builder.get();
        assertNotNull(attributes);
        assertEquals("", attributes.getDescription());
        assertEquals("", attributes.getSince());
        assertFalse(attributes.isForRemoval());
    }

    @Test
    public void testDefaultInstance() {
        DeprecatedAttributes attributes = DeprecatedAttributes.DEFAULT;
        assertEquals("", attributes.getDescription());
        assertEquals("", attributes.getSince());
        assertFalse(attributes.isForRemoval());
        assertEquals("Deprecated", attributes.toString());
    }

```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class GnuParserTest {

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_EmptyArguments() {
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_NullArguments() {
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, null, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_SingleDash() {
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"-"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_DoubleDash() {
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"--", "arg"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--", "arg"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_ExistingShortOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"-a"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-a"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_ExistingLongOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("long").build());
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"--long"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--long"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_OptionWithEqualSign_ValidOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("key").build());
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"--key=value"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--key", "value"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_OptionWithEqualSign_InvalidOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"--invalid=value"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--invalid=value"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_CompoundOption_ValidPrefix() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("ab").build());
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"-abc"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-ab", "c"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_CompoundOption_InvalidPrefix() {
        Options options = new Options();
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"-xyz"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-xyz"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_StopAtNonOption_True() {
        Options options = new Options();
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"-x", "arg1", "arg2"}, true);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-x", "arg1", "arg2"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_StopAtNonOption_False() {
        Options options = new Options();
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"-x", "arg1", "arg2"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-x", "arg1", "arg2"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_MixedArguments() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("bc").build());
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] arguments = {"-a", "--", "nonopt", "-bcd"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, arguments, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-a", "--", "nonopt", "-bcd"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_NullElementInArguments() {
        Options options = new Options();
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{null, "-a"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-a"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_AfterDoubleDash() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"--", "-a"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--", "-a"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testFlatten_NonOptionArgument() {
        Options options = new Options();
        GnuParser parser = new GnuParser();
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"arg"}, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"arg"}, result);
    }
}

生成测试代码：
```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.io.StringWriter;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.function.Function;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class HelpFormatterTest {

    private HelpFormatter formatter;
    private Options options;
    private StringWriter out;
    private PrintWriter printWriter;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        out = new StringWriter();
        printWriter = new PrintWriter(out);
        formatter = HelpFormatter.builder().setPrintWriter(printWriter).get();

        options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").longOpt("arg").desc("Description").build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("b").hasArg().argName("file").desc("File option").since("1.0").build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("c").deprecated().desc("Deprecated option").build());

        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.addOption(Option.builder("g1").build());
        group.addOption(Option.builder("g2").build());
        options.addOptionGroup(group);
    }

    @Test
    void testConstructor() {
        Function<Option, String> func = o -> "deprecated";
        HelpFormatter fmt = new HelpFormatter(func, printWriter, true);
        assertNotNull(fmt);
    }

    @Test
    void testAppendOption() throws Exception {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        Option option = Option.builder("t").longOpt("test").hasArg().argName("val").build();
        formatter.appendOption(sb, option, true);
        assertEquals("-t,--test <val>", sb.toString());
    }

    @Test
    void testAppendOptionGroup() throws Exception {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.addOption(Option.builder("x").build());
        group.addOption(Option.builder("y").build());
        formatter.appendOptionGroup(sb, group);
        assertEquals("[ -x | -y ]", sb.toString());
    }

    @Test
    void testAppendOptions() throws Exception {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        formatter.appendOptions(sb, 80, options, 2, 4);
        assertTrue(sb.toString().contains("-a,--arg"));
    }

    @Test
    void testAppendWrappedText() throws Exception {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        formatter.appendWrappedText(sb, 20, 5, "This is a long text that should wrap");
        assertTrue(sb.toString().contains("\n"));
    }

    @Test
    void testCreatePadding() {
        String padding = formatter.createPadding(5);
        assertEquals("     ", padding);
    }

    @Test
    void testDetermineMaxSinceLength() {
        HelpFormatter fmt = HelpFormatter.builder().setShowSince(true).get();
        int maxLen = fmt.determineMaxSinceLength(options);
        assertTrue(maxLen >= 3);
    }

    void testFindWrapPos() {
        String text = "This is a sample text for wrapping";
        int pos = formatter.findWrapPos(text, 10, 0);
        assertTrue(pos > 0 && pos < text.length());
    }

    @Test
    void testGettersAndSetters() {
        formatter.setArgName("param");
        assertEquals("param", formatter.getArgName());

        formatter.setDescPadding(5);
        assertEquals(5, formatter.getDescPadding());

        formatter.setLeftPadding(3);
        assertEquals(3, formatter.getLeftPadding());

        formatter.setLongOptPrefix("++");
        assertEquals("++", formatter.getLongOptPrefix());

        formatter.setLongOptSeparator("=");
        assertEquals("=", formatter.getLongOptSeparator());

        formatter.setNewLine("\r\n");
        assertEquals("\r\n", formatter.getNewLine());

        Comparator<Option> comp = (o1, o2) -> o1.getKey().compareTo(o2.getKey());
        formatter.setOptionComparator(comp);
        assertEquals(comp, formatter.getOptionComparator());

        formatter.setOptPrefix("+");
        assertEquals("+", formatter.getOptPrefix());

        formatter.setSyntaxPrefix("cmd: ");
        assertEquals("cmd: ", formatter.getSyntaxPrefix());

        formatter.setWidth(100);
        assertEquals(100, formatter.getWidth());
    }

        formatter.printHelp(80, "test", "Header", options, "Footer");
        printWriter.flush();
        assertTrue(out.toString().contains("Header"));

        printWriter.flush();
        assertFalse(out.toString().isEmpty());

public class MissingArgumentExceptionTest {

        Option option = Option.builder("testOption").build();
        MissingArgumentException exception = new MissingArgumentException(option);

        assertEquals("Missing argument for option: " + option.getKey(), exception.getMessage());
        assertSame(option, exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void testConstructorWithString() {
        String message = "Custom error message";
        MissingArgumentException exception = new MissingArgumentException(message);

        assertEquals(message, exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptionAfterStringConstructor() {
        MissingArgumentException exception = new MissingArgumentException("Another message");
        assertNull(exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void testOptionConstructorMessageFormat() {
        Option option = Option.builder("x").build();
        MissingArgumentException exception = new MissingArgumentException(option);

        assertTrue(exception.getMessage().contains(option.getKey()));
        assertTrue(exception.getMessage().startsWith("Missing argument for option: "));
    }

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

class MissingOptionExceptionTest {

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_singleOption() {
        List<String> missing = Collections.singletonList("file");
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals("Missing required option: file", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_multipleOptions() {
        List<String> missing = Arrays.asList("file", "output", "verbose");
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals("Missing required options: file, output, verbose", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_emptyList() {
        List<String> missing = Collections.emptyList();
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals("Missing required options: ", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithMessage() {
        String message = "Custom error message";
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(message);

        assertEquals(message, exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void getMissingOptions_afterListConstructor() {
        List<String> missing = Arrays.asList("host", "port");
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void getMissingOptions_afterMessageConstructor() {
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException("Error");
        assertNull(exception.getMissingOptions());
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

class MissingOptionExceptionTest {

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_singleOption() {
        List<String> missing = Collections.singletonList("file");
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals("Missing required option: file", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_multipleOptions() {
        List<String> missing = Arrays.asList("file", "output", "verbose");
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals("Missing required options: file, output, verbose", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_emptyList() {
        List<String> missing = Collections.emptyList();
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals("Missing required options: ", exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithList_null() {
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> new MissingOptionException((List) null));
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithMessage() {
        String message = "Custom error message";
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(message);

        assertEquals(message, exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorWithMessage_null() {
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException((String) null);
        assertNull(exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void getMissingOptions_afterListConstructor() {
        List<String> missing = Arrays.asList("host", "port");
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException(missing);

        assertEquals(missing, exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void getMissingOptions_afterMessageConstructor() {
        MissingOptionException exception = new MissingOptionException("Error");
        assertNull(exception.getMissingOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_singleOption() {
        List<String> missing = Collections.singletonList("file");
        String message = MissingOptionException.createMessage(missing);
        assertEquals("Missing required option: file", message);
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_multipleOptions() {
        List<String> missing = Arrays.asList("file", "output", "verbose");
        String message = MissingOptionException.createMessage(missing);
        assertEquals("Missing required options: file, output, verbose", message);
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_emptyList() {
        List<String> missing = Collections.emptyList();
        String message = MissingOptionException.createMessage(missing);
        assertEquals("Missing required options: ", message);
    }

    @Test
    void createMessage_nullList() {
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> MissingOptionException.createMessage(null));
    }
}

package cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class OptionTest {

    private Option option;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        option = Option.builder("t").build();
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderWithOptionValidation() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Option.builder(null));
    }

    @Test
    void testBuildWithMissingOption() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Option.builder().build());
    }

    @Test
    void testArgNameMethod() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("f").argName("file").build();
        assertEquals("file", opt.getArgName());
    }

    @Test
    void testConverterSetting() {
        Converter<?, ?> converter = new Converter<>();
        Option opt = Option.builder("c").converter(converter).build();
        assertSame(converter, opt.getConverter());
    }

    @Test
    void testDeprecatedMethods() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("d").deprecated().build();
        assertTrue(opt.isDeprecated());
    }

    @Test
    void testHasArgConfiguration() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").hasArg().build();
        assertTrue(opt.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testUnlimitedArgsConfiguration() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("m").hasArgs().build();
        assertTrue(opt.hasArgs());
    }

    @Test
    void testOptionalArgWithArgCount() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("o").optionalArg(true).build();
        assertTrue(opt.hasOptionalArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testRequiredConfiguration() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("r").required().build();
        assertTrue(opt.isRequired());
    }

    @Test
    void testValueSeparatorSetting() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("s").valueSeparator('=').build();
        assertEquals('=', opt.getValueSeparator());
    }

    @Test
    void testMultiParamConstructor() {
        Option opt = new Option("v", "verbose", false, "Enable verbose");
        assertEquals("verbose", opt.getLongOpt());
    }

    @Test
    void testAcceptsArgWhenUninitialized() {
        option.setArgs(Option.UNINITIALIZED);
        assertFalse(option.acceptsArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testAddValueBeyondCapacity() {
        option.setArgs(1);
        option.add("val1");
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> option.add("val2"));
    }

    @Test
    void testClearValuesOperation() {
        option.add("test");
        option.clearValues();
        assertTrue(option.hasNoValues());
    }

    @Test
    void testCloneEquality() {
        option.setArgName("clone");
        Option clone = (Option) option.clone();
        assertEquals(option, clone);
    }

    @Test
    void testValueRetrievalMethods() {
        option.processValue("value");
        assertEquals("value", option.getValue());
        assertEquals("value", option.getValue(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testSeparatedValueProcessing() {
        Option sepOption = Option.builder("D").valueSeparator('=').build();
        sepOption.processValue("key=value");
        assertEquals("key", sepOption.getValue(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testRequiresArgWithOptional() {
        option.setOptionalArg(true);
        assertFalse(option.requiresArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testTypeConversionHandling() {
        option.setType(Integer.class);
        assertSame(Integer.class, option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testToStringRepresentation() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("h").longOpt("help").desc("Show help").build();
        assertTrue(opt.toString().contains("help"));
    }

    @Test
    void testDeprecatedAttributesOutput() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("old").deprecated(DeprecatedAttributes.DEFAULT).build();
        assertFalse(opt.toDeprecatedString().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void testHashCodeConsistency() {
        Option opt1 = Option.builder("x").build();
        Option opt2 = Option.builder("x").build();
        assertEquals(opt1.hashCode(), opt2.hashCode());
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessValueWithoutSeparator() {
        option.setArgs(2);
        option.processValue("a,b");
        assertEquals(1, option.getValuesList().size());
    }

    @Test
    void testUnlimitedArgsAcceptance() {
        option.setArgs(Option.UNLIMITED_VALUES);
        assertTrue(option.acceptsArg());
    }
}

```java
package cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class OptionTest {

    private Option option;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        option = Option.builder("t").build();
    }

    @Test
    void testBuilderWithOptionValidation() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Option.builder(null));
    }

    @Test
    void testBuildWithMissingOption() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Option.builder().build());
    }

    @Test
    void testArgNameMethod() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("f").argName("file").build();
        assertEquals("file", opt.getArgName());
        assertTrue(opt.hasArgName());
    }

    @Test
    void testConverterSetting() {
        Converter<?, ?> converter = new Converter<>();
        Option opt = Option.builder("c").converter(converter).build();
        assertSame(converter, opt.getConverter());
    }

    @Test
    void testDeprecatedMethods() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("d").deprecated().build();
        assertTrue(opt.isDeprecated());
        assertNotNull(opt.getDeprecated());
    }

    @Test
    void testHasArgConfiguration() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").hasArg().build();
        assertTrue(opt.hasArg());

        Option opt2 = Option.builder("b").hasArg(false).build();
        assertFalse(opt2.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testUnlimitedArgsConfiguration() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("m").hasArgs().build();
        assertTrue(opt.hasArgs());
        assertEquals(Option.UNLIMITED_VALUES, opt.getArgs());
    }

    @Test
    void testOptionalArgWithArgCount() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("o").optionalArg(true).build();
        assertTrue(opt.hasOptionalArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testRequiredConfiguration() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("r").required().build();
        assertTrue(opt.isRequired());

        Option opt2 = Option.builder("s").required(false).build();
        assertFalse(opt2.isRequired());
    }

    @Test
    void testValueSeparatorSetting() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("s").valueSeparator('=').build();
        assertEquals('=', opt.getValueSeparator());
        assertTrue(opt.hasValueSeparator());
    }

    @Test
    void testMultiParamConstructor() {
        Option opt = new Option("v", "verbose", false, "Enable verbose");
        assertEquals("verbose", opt.getLongOpt());
        assertEquals("v", opt.getOpt());
        assertEquals("Enable verbose", opt.getDescription());
        assertTrue(opt.hasLongOpt());
    }

    @Test
    void testAcceptsArgWhenUninitialized() {
        option.setArgs(Option.UNINITIALIZED);
        assertFalse(option.acceptsArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testAddValueBeyondCapacity() {
        option.setArgs(1);
        option.add("val1");
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> option.add("val2"));
    }

    @Test
    void testClearValuesOperation() {
        option.add("test");
        option.clearValues();
        assertTrue(option.hasNoValues());
    }

    @Test
    void testCloneEquality() {
        option.setArgName("clone");
        Option clone = (Option) option.clone();
        assertEquals(option, clone);
    }

    @Test
    void testValueRetrievalMethods() {
        option.processValue("value");
        assertEquals("value", option.getValue());
        assertEquals("value", option.getValue(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testSeparatedValueProcessing() {
        Option sepOption = Option.builder("D").valueSeparator('=').build();
        sepOption.processValue("key=value");
        assertEquals("key", sepOption.getValue(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testRequiresArgWithOptional() {
        option.setOptionalArg(true);
        assertFalse(option.requiresArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testTypeConversionHandling() {
        option.setType(Integer.class);
        assertSame(Integer.class, option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testToStringRepresentation() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("h").longOpt("help").desc("Show help").build();
        assertTrue(opt.toString().contains("help"));
    }

    @Test
    void testDeprecatedAttributesOutput() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("old").deprecated(DeprecatedAttributes.DEFAULT).build();
        assertFalse(opt.toDeprecatedString().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void testHashCodeConsistency() {
        Option opt1 = Option.builder("x").build();
        Option opt2 = Option.builder("x").build();
        assertEquals(opt1.hashCode(), opt2.hashCode());
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessValueWithoutSeparator() {
        option.setArgs(2);
        option.processValue("a,b");
        assertEquals(1, option.getValuesList().size());
    }

    @Test
    void testUnlimitedArgsAcceptance() {
        option.setArgs(Option.UNLIMITED_VALUES);
        assertTrue(option.acceptsArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testToTypeMethod() {
        assertEquals(String.class, option.toType(String.class));
        assertEquals(String.class, option.toType(null));
    }

    @Test
    void testNumberOfArgsSetting() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("n").numberOfArgs(3).build();
        assertEquals(3, opt.getArgs());

        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Option.builder("n").numberOfArgs(-5));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> Option.builder("n").numberOfArgs(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testSinceVersion() {
        Option opt = Option.builder("v").since("1.2.3").build();
        assertEquals("1.2.3", opt.getSince());
    }

    @Test
    void testAddValueMethod() {
        assertTrue(option.addValue("test"));
        assertEquals("test", option.getValue());
    }

    @Test
    void testGetKeyAndId() {
        assertEquals(116, option.getId());
        assertEquals("t", option.getKey());
    }

    @Test
    void testSetMethods() {
        option.setConverter(new Converter<>());
        assertNotNull(option.getConverter());

        option.setDescription("new desc");
        assertEquals("new desc", option.getDescription());

        option.setLongOpt("long");
        assertEquals("long", option.getLongOpt());
        assertTrue(option.hasLongOpt());

        option.setRequired(true);
        assertTrue(option.isRequired());

        option.setValueSeparator(',');
        assertEquals(',', option.getValueSeparator());
        assertTrue(option.hasValueSeparator());
    }
}

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.lang.reflect.*;

public class OptionBuilderTest {

    @AfterEach
    public void tearDown() {
        OptionBuilder.reset();
    }

    @Test
    public void testOptionBuilderConstructorIsPrivate() throws Exception {
        Constructor<OptionBuilder> constructor = OptionBuilder.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        assertFalse(Modifier.isPublic(constructor.getModifiers()));
        constructor.setAccessible(true);
        constructor.newInstance();
    }

    @Test
    public void testCreateWithoutLongOptThrowsException() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> OptionBuilder.create());
    }

        assertEquals("long", option.getLongOpt());
        assertNull(option.getOpt());

        assertEquals(0, option.getArgs());

```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.util.Collection;

class OptionGroupTest {

    @Test
    void testConstructor() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        assertNotNull(group);
        assertTrue(group.getNames().isEmpty());
        assertFalse(group.isSelected());
        assertNull(group.getSelected());
        assertFalse(group.isRequired());
    }

    @Test
    void testAddOption() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option option = Option.builder("test").build();
        OptionGroup result = group.addOption(option);
        assertSame(group, result);
        assertEquals(1, group.getNames().size());
        assertTrue(group.getNames().contains("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetNames() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option option1 = Option.builder("opt1").build();
        Option option2 = Option.builder("opt2").build();
        group.addOption(option1).addOption(option2);
        Collection<String> names = group.getNames();
        assertEquals(2, names.size());
        assertTrue(names.contains("opt1"));
        assertTrue(names.contains("opt2"));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetOptions() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option option1 = Option.builder("opt1").build();
        Option option2 = Option.builder("opt2").build();
        group.addOption(option1).addOption(option2);
        Collection<Option> options = group.getOptions();
        assertEquals(2, options.size());
        assertTrue(options.contains(option1));
        assertTrue(options.contains(option2));
    }

    @Test
    void testSetSelected() throws AlreadySelectedException {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option option1 = Option.builder("opt1").build();
        Option option2 = Option.builder("opt2").build();
        group.addOption(option1).addOption(option2);

        group.setSelected(option1);
        assertTrue(group.isSelected());
        assertEquals("opt1", group.getSelected());

        group.setSelected(option1);
        assertEquals("opt1", group.getSelected());

        assertThrows(AlreadySelectedException.class, () -> group.setSelected(option2));
    }

    @Test
    void testSetSelectedNull() throws AlreadySelectedException {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option option = Option.builder("test").build();
        group.addOption(option);
        group.setSelected(option);

        group.setSelected(null);
        assertFalse(group.isSelected());
        assertNull(group.getSelected());
    }

    @Test
    void testSetRequired() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.setRequired(true);
        assertTrue(group.isRequired());

        group.setRequired(false);
        assertFalse(group.isRequired());
    }

    @Test
    void testIsSelected() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        assertFalse(group.isSelected());

        Option option = Option.builder("test").build();
        group.addOption(option);
        try {
            group.setSelected(option);
        } catch (AlreadySelectedException e) {
            fail("Unexpected exception");
        }
        assertTrue(group.isSelected());
    }

    @Test
    void testToString() {
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option option1 = Option.builder("opt1").opt('a').build();
        Option option2 = Option.builder("opt2").longOpt("long2").build();
        Option option3 = Option.builder("opt3").longOpt("long3").desc("Desc").build();

        group.addOption(option1).addOption(option2).addOption(option3);

        String result = group.toString();
        assertTrue(result.contains("[-a]"));
        assertTrue(result.contains("[--long2]"));
        assertTrue(result.contains("[--long3 Desc]"));
        assertTrue(result.contains(", "));
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class OptionsTest {

    @Test
    public void testConstructor() {
        Options options = new Options();
        assertNotNull(options);
        assertTrue(options.getOptions().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddOptionWithOptionObject() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").build();
        Options result = options.addOption(opt);
        assertSame(options, result);
        assertTrue(options.hasOption("a"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddShortOptionWithArg() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Options result = options.addOption("b", true, "description");
        assertSame(options, result);
        assertTrue(options.hasOption("b"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddShortOptionWithoutArg() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Options result = options.addOption("c", "description");
        assertSame(options, result);
        assertTrue(options.hasOption("c"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddFullOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Options result = options.addOption("d", "longD", true, "description");
        assertSame(options, result);
        assertTrue(options.hasOption("d"));
        assertTrue(options.hasLongOption("longD"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddOptionGroup() {
        Options options = new Options();
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option opt1 = Option.builder("e").build();
        Option opt2 = Option.builder("f").build();
        group.addOption(opt1);
        group.addOption(opt2);
        Options result = options.addOptionGroup(group);
        assertSame(options, result);
        assertSame(group, options.getOptionGroup(opt1));
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddOptions() {
        Options options1 = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("g").build();
        options1.addOption(opt);

        Options options2 = new Options();
        Options result = options2.addOptions(options1);
        assertSame(options2, result);
        assertTrue(options2.hasOption("g"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddRequiredOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Options result = options.addRequiredOption("h", "longH", true, "required");
        assertSame(options, result);
        assertTrue(options.getRequiredOptions().contains("h"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetMatchingOptions() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("i", "longI", false, "desc");
        options.addOption("j", "longJ", false, "desc");

        List<String> matches = options.getMatchingOptions("longI");
        assertEquals(1, matches.size());
        assertEquals("longI", matches.get(0));

        List<String> prefixMatches = options.getMatchingOptions("long");
        assertEquals(2, prefixMatches.size());
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("k").longOpt("longK").build();
        options.addOption(opt);

        Option resultShort = options.getOption("k");
        assertSame(opt, resultShort);

        Option resultLong = options.getOption("longK");
        assertSame(opt, resultLong);

        assertNull(options.getOption("invalid"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetOptionGroup() {
        Options options = new Options();
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        Option opt = Option.builder("l").build();
        group.addOption(opt);
        options.addOptionGroup(group);

        assertSame(group, options.getOptionGroup(opt));
        assertNull(options.getOptionGroup(Option.builder("m").build()));
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetOptionGroups() {
        Options options = new Options();
        OptionGroup group1 = new OptionGroup();
        OptionGroup group2 = new OptionGroup();
        options.addOptionGroup(group1);
        options.addOptionGroup(group2);

        Collection<OptionGroup> groups = options.getOptionGroups();
        assertEquals(2, groups.size());
        assertTrue(groups.contains(group1));
        assertTrue(groups.contains(group2));
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetOptions() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt1 = Option.builder("n").build();
        Option opt2 = Option.builder("o").build();
        options.addOption(opt1);
        options.addOption(opt2);

        Collection<Option> result = options.getOptions();
        assertEquals(2, result.size());
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> result.add(Option.builder("p").build()));
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetRequiredOptions() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addRequiredOption("q", "longQ", true, "required");

        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.setRequired(true);
        Option opt = Option.builder("r").build();
        group.addOption(opt);
        options.addOptionGroup(group);

        List<?> required = options.getRequiredOptions();
        assertEquals(2, required.size());
        assertTrue(required.contains("q"));
        assertTrue(required.contains(group));
    }

    @Test
    public void testHasLongOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("s", "longS", false, "desc");
        assertTrue(options.hasLongOption("longS"));
        assertFalse(options.hasLongOption("invalid"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testHasOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("t", "longT", false, "desc");
        assertTrue(options.hasOption("t"));
        assertTrue(options.hasOption("longT"));
        assertFalse(options.hasOption("invalid"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testHasShortOption() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("u", "longU", false, "desc");
        assertTrue(options.hasShortOption("u"));
        assertFalse(options.hasShortOption("longU"));
        assertFalse(options.hasShortOption("invalid"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testHelpOptions() {
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("v").build();
        options.addOption(opt);

        List<Option> helpOpts = options.helpOptions();
        assertEquals(1, helpOpts.size());
        assertSame(opt, helpOpts.get(0));
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString() {
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("w", "desc");
        String result = options.toString();
        assertTrue(result.contains("Options: [ short"));
        assertTrue(result.contains("w"));
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;

public class OptionValidatorTest {

    @Test
    public void testIsValidChar_ValidJavaIdentifier() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("isValidChar", char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, 'a');
        Assertions.assertTrue(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIsValidChar_AdditionalLongChar() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("isValidChar", char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, '-');
        Assertions.assertTrue(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIsValidChar_InvalidChar() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("isValidChar", char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, '!');
        Assertions.assertFalse(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIsValidOpt_ValidJavaIdentifier() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("isValidOpt", char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, 'b');
        Assertions.assertTrue(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIsValidOpt_AdditionalOptionChar() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("isValidOpt", char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, '?');
        Assertions.assertTrue(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIsValidOpt_InvalidChar() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("isValidOpt", char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, '&');
        Assertions.assertFalse(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSearch_CharFound() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("search", char[].class, char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, new char[]{'x', 'y', 'z'}, 'y');
        Assertions.assertTrue(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSearch_CharNotFound() throws Exception {
        Method method = OptionValidator.class.getDeclaredMethod("search", char[].class, char.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);
        boolean result = (boolean) method.invoke(null, new char[]{'x', 'y', 'z'}, 'a');
        Assertions.assertFalse(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_NullOption() {
        String result = OptionValidator.validate(null);
        Assertions.assertNull(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_EmptyOption() {
        Exception exception = Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> {
            OptionValidator.validate("");
        });
        Assertions.assertEquals("Empty option name.", exception.getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_InvalidFirstChar() {
        Exception exception = Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> {
            OptionValidator.validate("&test");
        });
        Assertions.assertTrue(exception.getMessage().contains("Illegal option name"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_ValidSingleChar() {
        String result = OptionValidator.validate("a");
        Assertions.assertEquals("a", result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_InvalidSubsequentChar() {
        Exception exception = Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> {
            OptionValidator.validate("a*b");
        });
        Assertions.assertTrue(exception.getMessage().contains("contains an illegal"));
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_ValidMultiChar() {
        String result = OptionValidator.validate("valid-option");
        Assertions.assertEquals("valid-option", result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testValidate_ValidWithAdditionalChars() {
        String result = OptionValidator.validate("?option@");
        Assertions.assertEquals("?option@", result);
    }
}

```java
package cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

public class ParseExceptionTest {

    @Test
    public void testWrapWithUnsupportedOperationException() {
        UnsupportedOperationException ex = new UnsupportedOperationException();
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> ParseException.wrap(ex));
    }

    @Test
    public void testWrapWithParseException() {
        ParseException ex = new ParseException("Test");
        ParseException result = ParseException.wrap(ex);
        assertSame(ex, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testWrapWithOtherException() {
        RuntimeException ex = new RuntimeException("Test");
        ParseException result = ParseException.wrap(ex);
        assertNotNull(result);
        assertSame(ex, result.getCause());
    }

    @Test
    public void testConstructorWithMessage() {
        String message = "Test message";
        ParseException ex = new ParseException(message);
        assertEquals(message, ex.getMessage());
        assertNull(ex.getCause());
    }

    @Test
    public void testConstructorWithThrowable() {
        Throwable cause = new RuntimeException("Cause");
        ParseException ex = new ParseException(cause);
        assertSame(cause, ex.getCause());
        assertEquals(cause.toString(), ex.getMessage());
    }
}

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.function.Executable;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ListIterator;
import java.util.Properties;

public class ParserTest {

    @Test
    void testParserConstructor() {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        assertNotNull(parser);
    }

    @Test
    void testSetAndGetOptions() {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        parser.setOptions(options);
        assertSame(options, parser.getOptions());
    }

    @Test
    void testGetRequiredOptionsInitialState() {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        parser.setOptions(options);
        assertTrue(parser.getRequiredOptions().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void testCheckRequiredOptionsThrowsWhenMissing() {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addRequiredOption("a", "aaa", false, "");
        parser.setOptions(options);

        MissingOptionException exception = assertThrows(MissingOptionException.class, () -> {
            parser.checkRequiredOptions();
        });
        assertTrue(exception.getMissingOptions().contains("a"));
    }

    @Test
    void testCheckRequiredOptionsNoExceptionWhenSatisfied() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").required(true).build();
        options.addOption(opt);
        parser.setOptions(options);

        Method updateMethod = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("updateRequiredOptions", Option.class);
        updateMethod.setAccessible(true);
        updateMethod.invoke(parser, opt);

        assertDoesNotThrow((Executable) parser::checkRequiredOptions);
    }

    @Test
    void testUpdateRequiredOptionsRemovesOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").required(true).build();
        options.addOption(opt);
        parser.setOptions(options);

        Method updateMethod = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("updateRequiredOptions", Option.class);
        updateMethod.setAccessible(true);
        updateMethod.invoke(parser, opt);

        assertFalse(parser.getRequiredOptions().contains("a"));
    }

    @Test
    void testUpdateRequiredOptionsRemovesGroup() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        OptionGroup group = new OptionGroup();
        group.setRequired(true);
        Option opt1 = Option.builder("a").build();
        Option opt2 = Option.builder("b").build();
        group.addOption(opt1);
        group.addOption(opt2);
        options.addOptionGroup(group);
        parser.setOptions(options);

        Method updateMethod = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("updateRequiredOptions", Option.class);
        updateMethod.setAccessible(true);
        updateMethod.invoke(parser, opt1);

        assertFalse(parser.getRequiredOptions().contains(group));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseSimpleOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("a", false, "");
        String[] args = {"-a"};

        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, args);
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseOptionWithArgument() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").hasArg(true).build());
        String[] args = {"-a", "value"};

        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, args);
        assertEquals("value", cmd.getOptionValue("a"));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseStopAtNonOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("a", false, "");
        String[] args = {"-a", "nonOption", "-b"};

        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, args, true);
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
        assertEquals(1, cmd.getArgList().size());
        assertEquals("nonOption", cmd.getArgList().get(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseDoubleDashStopsProcessing() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("a", false, "");
        String[] args = {"-a", "--", "-b"};

        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, args);
        assertTrue(cmd.hasOption("a"));
        assertEquals(1, cmd.getArgList().size());
        assertEquals("-b", cmd.getArgList().get(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseSingleDashAsArgumentWhenNotStopping() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        String[] args = {"-"};

        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, args);
        assertEquals(1, cmd.getArgList().size());
        assertEquals("-", cmd.getArgList().get(0));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseSingleDashStopsProcessingWhenStopAtNonOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption("a", false, "");
        String[] args = {"-", "-a"};

        CommandLine cmd = parser.parse(options, args, true);
        assertEquals(2, cmd.getArgList().size());
        assertFalse(cmd.hasOption("a"));
    }

    @Test
    void testParseUnrecognizedOptionThrows() {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        String[] args = {"-unknown"};

        assertThrows(UnrecognizedOptionException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, args);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void testParseMissingArgumentThrows() {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").hasArg(true).build());
        String[] args = {"-a"};

        assertThrows(MissingArgumentException.class, () -> {
            parser.parse(options, args);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessPropertiesAddsDefaultOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").build();
        options.addOption(opt);
        parser.setOptions(options);

        Properties properties = new Properties();
        properties.setProperty("a", "true");

        Method processProps = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processProperties", Properties.class);
        processProps.setAccessible(true);
        processProps.invoke(parser, properties);

        parser.parse(options, new String[0]);
        assertTrue(parser.cmd.hasOption("a"));
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessPropertiesThrowsForUndefinedOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        parser.setOptions(options);

        Properties properties = new Properties();
        properties.setProperty("b", "true");

        Method processProps = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processProperties", Properties.class);
        processProps.setAccessible(true);

        assertThrows(UnrecognizedOptionException.class, () -> {
            processProps.invoke(parser, properties);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessArgsHandlesMultipleValues() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        parser.setOptions(options);
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").hasArgs().build();
        List<String> tokens = Arrays.asList("v1", "v2", "-b");
        ListIterator<String> iter = tokens.listIterator();

        Method processArgs = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processArgs", Option.class, ListIterator.class);
        processArgs.setAccessible(true);
        processArgs.invoke(parser, opt, iter);

        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"v1", "v2"}, opt.getValues());
        assertEquals("-b", iter.next());
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessArgsThrowsWhenMissingValue() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        parser.setOptions(options);
        Option opt = Option.builder("a").hasArg(true).build();
        List<String> tokens = new ArrayList<>();
        ListIterator<String> iter = tokens.listIterator();

        Method processArgs = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processArgs", Option.class, ListIterator.class);
        processArgs.setAccessible(true);

        assertThrows(MissingArgumentException.class, () -> {
            processArgs.invoke(parser, opt, iter);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessOptionThrowsForUnrecognizedOption() throws Exception {
        DefaultParser parser = new DefaultParser();
        Options options = new Options();
        parser.setOptions(options);

        Method processOption = Parser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processOption", String.class, ListIterator.class);
        processOption.setAccessible(true);
        ListIterator<String> iter = new ArrayList<String>().listIterator();

        assertThrows(UnrecognizedOptionException.class, () -> {
            processOption.invoke(parser, "-unknown", iter);
        });
    }

    private static class DefaultParser extends Parser {
        @Override
        protected String[] flatten(Options opts, String[] arguments, boolean stopAtNonOption) {
            return arguments;
        }
    }
}
