package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class PatternOptionBuilderTest {

    @Test
    void testGetValueTypeAllCases() {
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.OBJECT_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('@'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType(':'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.NUMBER_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('%'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.CLASS_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('+'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.DATE_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('#'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.EXISTING_FILE_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('<'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.FILE_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('>'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.FILES_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('*'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.URL_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('/'));
        assertNull(PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('x'));
    }

    @Test
    void testIsValueCodeValidCodes() {
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('@'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode(':'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('%'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('+'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('#'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('<'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('>'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('*'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('/'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('!'));
    }

    @Test
    void testIsValueCodeInvalidCodes() {
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('a'));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('Z'));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('1'));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode(' '));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('-'));
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternEmptyString() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("");
        assertEquals(0, options.getOptions().size());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternSingleOption() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertFalse(option.hasArg());
        assertFalse(option.isRequired());
        assertNull(option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternOptionWithValueCode() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a:");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertTrue(option.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, option.getType());
        assertFalse(option.isRequired());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternRequiredFlag() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a!");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertFalse(option.hasArg());
        assertTrue(option.isRequired());
        assertNull(option.getType());
    }

    @Test

        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a:!");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertTrue(option.hasArg());
        assertTrue(option.isRequired());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternMultipleOptions() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("ab:c");
        assertEquals(3, options.getOptions().size());

        Option optionA = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(optionA);
        assertFalse(optionA.hasArg());

        Option optionB = options.getOption("b");
        assertNotNull(optionB);
        assertTrue(optionB.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, optionB.getType());

        Option optionC = options.getOption("c");
        assertNotNull(optionC);
        assertFalse(optionC.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternTrailingValueCode() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a*b");
        Option optionA = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(optionA);
        assertTrue(optionA.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.FILES_VALUE, optionA.getType());

        Option optionB = options.getOption("b");
        assertNotNull(optionB);
        assertFalse(optionB.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternUnsupportedConverter() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a*");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        Converter<?, ?> converter = option.getConverter();
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> converter.convert("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void testUnsupportedConverter() {
        Converter<?, ?> converter = PatternOptionBuilder.unsupported();
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> converter.convert("test"));
    }

package cli;

import org.apache.commons.cli.Option;
import org.apache.commons.cli.Options;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import org.apache.commons.cli.converters.ArrayConverter;

public class PatternOptionBuilderTest {

    @Test
    void testConstructor() {
        new PatternOptionBuilder();
    }

    @Test
    void testGetValueClass() {
        assertEquals(String.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass(':'));
        assertEquals(Number.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('%'));
        assertEquals(Class.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('+'));
        assertEquals(java.util.Date.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('#'));
        assertEquals(java.io.File.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('<'));
        assertEquals(java.io.File.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('>'));
        assertEquals(ArrayConverter.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('*'));
        assertEquals(java.net.URL.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('/'));
        assertEquals(Object.class, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('@'));
        assertNull(PatternOptionBuilder.getValueClass('x'));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetValueTypeAllCases() {
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.OBJECT_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('@'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType(':'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.NUMBER_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('%'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.CLASS_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('+'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.DATE_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('#'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.EXISTING_FILE_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('<'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.FILE_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('>'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.FILES_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('*'));
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.URL_VALUE, PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('/'));
        assertNull(PatternOptionBuilder.getValueType('x'));
    }

    @Test
    void testIsValueCodeValidCodes() {
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('@'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode(':'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('%'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('+'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('#'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('<'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('>'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('*'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('/'));
        assertTrue(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('!'));
    }

    @Test
    void testIsValueCodeInvalidCodes() {
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('a'));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('Z'));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('1'));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode(' '));
        assertFalse(PatternOptionBuilder.isValueCode('-'));
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternEmptyString() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("");
        assertEquals(0, options.getOptions().size());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternSingleOption() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertFalse(option.hasArg());
        assertFalse(option.isRequired());
        assertNull(option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternOptionWithValueCode() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a:");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertTrue(option.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, option.getType());
        assertFalse(option.isRequired());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternRequiredFlag() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a!");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertFalse(option.hasArg());
        assertTrue(option.isRequired());
        assertNull(option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternCombinedValueAndRequired() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a:!");
        Option option = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(option);
        assertTrue(option.hasArg());
        assertTrue(option.isRequired());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, option.getType());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternMultipleOptions() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("ab:c");
        assertEquals(3, options.getOptions().size());

        Option optionA = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(optionA);
        assertFalse(optionA.hasArg());

        Option optionB = options.getOption("b");
        assertNotNull(optionB);
        assertTrue(optionB.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, optionB.getType());

        Option optionC = options.getOption("c");
        assertNotNull(optionC);
        assertFalse(optionC.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternTrailingValueCode() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a*b");
        Option optionA = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(optionA);
        assertTrue(optionA.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.FILES_VALUE, optionA.getType());

        Option optionB = options.getOption("b");
        assertNotNull(optionB);
        assertFalse(optionB.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testParsePatternConsecutiveValueCodes() {
        Options options = PatternOptionBuilder.parsePattern("a::");
        Option optionA = options.getOption("a");
        assertNotNull(optionA);
        assertTrue(optionA.hasArg());
        assertEquals(PatternOptionBuilder.STRING_VALUE, optionA.getType());

        Option optionColon = options.getOption(":");
        assertNotNull(optionColon);
        assertFalse(optionColon.hasArg());
    }

    @Test
    void testUnsupportedConverter() {
        Converter<?, ?> converter = PatternOptionBuilder.unsupported();
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class, () -> converter.convert("test"));
    }

package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;

public class PosixParserTest {

    private PosixParser parser;
    private Options options;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        parser = new PosixParser();
        options = new Options();
        options.addOption(Option.builder("a").build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("b").hasArg().build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("c").build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("long").longOpt("long").build());
    }

    @Test
    void testConstructor() {
        assertNotNull(new PosixParser());
    }

    @Test
    void testInit() throws Exception {
        Method initMethod = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("init");
        initMethod.setAccessible(true);

        parser.flatten(options, new String[]{"arg"}, false);
        initMethod.invoke(parser);

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertTrue(tokens.isEmpty());

        Field eatTheRestField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("eatTheRest");
        eatTheRestField.setAccessible(true);
        assertFalse((boolean) eatTheRestField.get(parser));
    }

    @Test
    void testGobble() throws Exception {
        Method gobbleMethod = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("gobble", Iterator.class);
        gobbleMethod.setAccessible(true);

        Field eatTheRestField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("eatTheRest");
        eatTheRestField.setAccessible(true);
        eatTheRestField.set(parser, true);

        List<String> input = Arrays.asList("arg1", "arg2");
        gobbleMethod.invoke(parser, input.iterator());

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(Arrays.asList("arg1", "arg2"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessNonOptionToken_StopAtNonOption() throws Exception {
        Method method = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processNonOptionToken", String.class, boolean.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);

        method.invoke(parser, "arg", true);

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(List.of("arg"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessNonOptionToken_StopWithCurrentOption() throws Exception {
        Method method = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processNonOptionToken", String.class, boolean.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);

        Field currentOptionField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("currentOption");
        currentOptionField.setAccessible(true);
        currentOptionField.set(parser, options.getOption("b"));

        method.invoke(parser, "arg", true);

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(List.of("arg"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testProcessOptionToken_StopAtNonOption() throws Exception {
        Method method = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("processOptionToken", String.class, boolean.class);
        method.setAccessible(true);

        method.invoke(parser, "-x", true);

        Field eatTheRestField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("eatTheRest");
        eatTheRestField.setAccessible(true);
        assertTrue((boolean) eatTheRestField.get(parser));

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(List.of("-x"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testBurstToken_ValidOption() throws Exception {
        Method burstMethod = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("burstToken", String.class, boolean.class);
        burstMethod.setAccessible(true);

        burstMethod.invoke(parser, "-abc", false);

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(Arrays.asList("-a", "-b", "-c"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testBurstToken_StopAtNonOption() throws Exception {
        Method burstMethod = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("burstToken", String.class, boolean.class);
        burstMethod.setAccessible(true);

        burstMethod.invoke(parser, "-ax", true);

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(List.of("-a"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testBurstToken_WithArgValue() throws Exception {
        Method burstMethod = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredMethod("burstToken", String.class, boolean.class);
        burstMethod.setAccessible(true);

        burstMethod.invoke(parser, "-bvalue", false);

        Field tokensField = PosixParser.class.getDeclaredField("tokens");
        tokensField.setAccessible(true);
        List<?> tokens = (List<?>) tokensField.get(parser);
        assertEquals(Arrays.asList("-b", "value"), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_SingleHyphen() {
        String[] args = {"-"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_DoubleHyphen() {
        String[] args = {"--"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_ShortOption() {
        String[] args = {"-a"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-a"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_LongOption() {
        String[] args = {"--long"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"--long"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_NonOptionToken() {
        String[] args = {"arg"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"arg"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_BurstToken() {
        String[] args = {"-abc"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, false);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-a", "-b", "-c"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_StopAtNonOption() {
        String[] args = {"-a", "arg", "-b"};
        String[] result = parser.flatten(options, args, true);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"-a", "--", "arg", "-b"}, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testFlatten_AmbiguousOption() {
        options.addOption(Option.builder("t").longOpt("test1").build());
        options.addOption(Option.builder("t").longOpt("test2").build());

        String[] args = {"-test"};
        assertThrows(AmbiguousOptionException.class, () -> parser.flatten(options, args, false));
    }
}

```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.BigInteger;
import java.net.MalformedURLException;
import java.net.URL;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TypeHandlerTest {

    @Test
    void testCreateClassValid() throws Exception {
        Class<?> result = TypeHandler.createClass("java.lang.String");
        assertEquals(String.class, result);
    }

    @Test
    void testCreateClassInvalid() {
        assertThrows(Exception.class, () -> TypeHandler.createClass("invalid.ClassName"));
    }

    @Test
    void testCreateClassEmpty() {
        assertThrows(Exception.class, () -> TypeHandler.createClass(""));
    }

    @Test
    void testCreateDateValid() {
        Date result = TypeHandler.createDate("2023-01-01");
        assertNotNull(result);
    }

    @Test
    void testCreateDateInvalid() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TypeHandler.createDate("invalid-date"));
    }

    @Test
    void testCreateDateEmpty() {

            assertNotNull(fis);
        }

        assertThrows(Exception.class, () -> TypeHandler.openFile("non_existent_file.txt"));
    }

        TypeHandler.putDefaultMap(customMap);
        assertTrue(customMap.containsKey(Date.class));
        assertTrue(customMap.containsKey(File.class));

    @Test
    void testConstructorDefault() {
        TypeHandler handler = new TypeHandler();
        Converter<Date, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(Date.class);
        assertNotNull(converter);
    }

    @Test
    void testConstructorCustomMap() {
        Map<Class<?>, Converter<?, ? extends Throwable>> customMap = new HashMap<>();
        customMap.put(String.class, s -> "custom_" + s);
        TypeHandler handler = new TypeHandler(customMap);
        Converter<String, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(String.class);
        assertEquals("custom_test", converter.apply("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetConverterExisting() {
        TypeHandler handler = TypeHandler.getDefault();
        Converter<Integer, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(Integer.class);
        assertNotNull(converter);
        assertEquals(123, converter.apply("123"));
    }

    @Test
    void testGetConverterDefaultFallback() {
        Map<Class<?>, Converter<?, ? extends Throwable>> customMap = new HashMap<>();
        TypeHandler handler = new TypeHandler(customMap);
        Converter<Object, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(Object.class);
        assertSame(Converter.OBJECT, converter);
    }

    @Test
    void testCharacterConverterHex() {
        TypeHandler handler = TypeHandler.getDefault();
        Converter<Character, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(Character.class);
        char result = converter.apply("\\u0041");
        assertEquals('A', result);
    }

    @Test
    void testCharacterConverterSingleChar() {
        TypeHandler handler = TypeHandler.getDefault();
        Converter<Character, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(Character.class);
        char result = converter.apply("B");
        assertEquals('B', result);
    }

    @Test
    void testCharacterConverterInvalid() {
        TypeHandler handler = TypeHandler.getDefault();
        Converter<Character, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(Character.class);
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> converter.apply(""));

    @Test
    void testBigIntegerConversion() {
        TypeHandler handler = TypeHandler.getDefault();
        Converter<BigInteger, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(BigInteger.class);
        BigInteger result = converter.apply("1234567890");
        assertEquals(new BigInteger("1234567890"), result);
    }

    @Test
    void testBigDecimalConversion() {
        TypeHandler handler = TypeHandler.getDefault();
        Converter<BigDecimal, ?> converter = handler.getConverter(BigDecimal.class);
        BigDecimal result = converter.apply("123.456");
        assertEquals(new BigDecimal("123.456"), result);
    }

```java
package cli;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class UnrecognizedOptionExceptionTest {

    @Test
    void constructorSingleParamSetsMessageAndNullOption() {
        String testMessage = "Test message";
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException(testMessage);
        assertEquals(testMessage, exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorDoubleParamSetsMessageWithNonNullOption() {
        String testMessage = "Test message";
        String testOption = "option";
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException(testMessage, testOption);
        assertEquals(testMessage, exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(testOption, exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorDoubleParamSetsMessageWithNullOption() {
        String testMessage = "Test message";
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException(testMessage, null);
        assertEquals(testMessage, exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorDoubleParamHandlesEmptyOption() {
        String testMessage = "Test message";
        String testOption = "";
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException(testMessage, testOption);
        assertEquals(testMessage, exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(testOption, exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void constructorDoubleParamHandlesNullMessage() {
        String testOption = "option";
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException(null, testOption);
        assertNull(exception.getMessage());
        assertEquals(testOption, exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void getOptionReturnsNullWhenNoOptionProvided() {
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException("Message");
        assertNull(exception.getOption());
    }

    @Test
    void getOptionReturnsCorrectOptionAfterDoubleParamConstructor() {
        String testOption = "testOption";
        UnrecognizedOptionException exception = new UnrecognizedOptionException("Message", testOption);
        assertEquals(testOption, exception.getOption());
    }
}

package cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class UtilTest {

    @Test
    void isEmptyObjectArray_NullInput_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty((Object[]) null));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyObjectArray_EmptyArray_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty(Util.EMPTY_STRING_ARRAY));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyObjectArray_NonEmptyArray_ReturnsFalse() {
        assertFalse(Util.isEmpty(new Object[]{"test"}));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyString_NullInput_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty((String) null));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyString_EmptyString_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty(""));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyString_NonEmptyString_ReturnsFalse() {
        assertFalse(Util.isEmpty("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_ValidStripCase_ReturnsStripped() {
        assertEquals("value", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\"value\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_InternalQuotes_NoStrip() {
        assertEquals("\"va\"lue\"", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\"va\"lue\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_SingleChar_NoStrip() {
        assertEquals("\"", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_NoQuotes_OriginalString() {
        assertEquals("value", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("value"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_DoubleHyphen_RemovesBoth() {
        assertEquals("option", Util.stripLeadingHyphens("--option"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_SingleHyphen_RemovesOne() {
        assertEquals("option", Util.stripLeadingHyphens("-option"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_NoHyphen_OriginalString() {
        assertEquals("option", Util.stripLeadingHyphens("option"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_EmptyString_ReturnsEmpty() {
        assertEquals("", Util.stripLeadingHyphens(""));
    }

    @Test
    void constructor_IsPrivate_ThrowsException() throws Exception {
        var constructor = Util.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        assertFalse(constructor.isAccessible());
        constructor.setAccessible(true);
        assertThrows(Exception.class, constructor::newInstance);
    }

```java
package cli;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;

class UtilTest {

    @Test
    void isEmptyObjectArray_NullInput_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty((Object[]) null));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyObjectArray_EmptyArray_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty(new Object[0]));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyObjectArray_NonEmptyArray_ReturnsFalse() {
        assertFalse(Util.isEmpty(new Object[]{"test"}));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyString_NullInput_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty((String) null));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyString_EmptyString_ReturnsTrue() {
        assertTrue(Util.isEmpty(""));
    }

    @Test
    void isEmptyString_NonEmptyString_ReturnsFalse() {
        assertFalse(Util.isEmpty("test"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_ValidStripCase_ReturnsStripped() {
        assertEquals("value", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\"value\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_InternalQuotes_NoStrip() {
        assertEquals("\"va\"lue\"", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\"va\"lue\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_SingleChar_NoStrip() {
        assertEquals("\"", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_NoQuotes_OriginalString() {
        assertEquals("value", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("value"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_NullInput_ReturnsNull() {
        assertNull(Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes(null));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_OnlyLeadingQuote_ReturnsOriginal() {
        assertEquals("\"value", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("\"value"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes_OnlyTrailingQuote_ReturnsOriginal() {
        assertEquals("value\"", Util.stripLeadingAndTrailingQuotes("value\""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_DoubleHyphen_RemovesBoth() {
        assertEquals("option", Util.stripLeadingHyphens("--option"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_SingleHyphen_RemovesOne() {
        assertEquals("option", Util.stripLeadingHyphens("-option"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_NoHyphen_OriginalString() {
        assertEquals("option", Util.stripLeadingHyphens("option"));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_EmptyString_ReturnsEmpty() {
        assertEquals("", Util.stripLeadingHyphens(""));
    }

    @Test
    void stripLeadingHyphens_NullInput_ReturnsNull() {
        assertNull(Util.stripLeadingHyphens(null));
    }

    @Test
    void constructor_IsPrivate_ThrowsException() throws Exception {
        var constructor = Util.class.getDeclaredConstructor();
        assertFalse(constructor.canAccess(null));
        constructor.setAccessible(true);
        Throwable exception = assertThrows(InvocationTargetException.class, constructor::newInstance);
        assertEquals(UnsupportedOperationException.class, exception.getCause().getClass());
    }
}
